[\n'desloppify/app/commands/helpers/queue_progress.py',\n'desloppify/app/commands/plan/cmd.py',\n'desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan.py',\n'desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_workflow.py',\n'desloppify/engine/_plan/reconcile.py',\n'desloppify/engine/_work_queue/context.py',\n'desloppify/app/commands/helpers/rendering.py',\n'desloppify/app/commands/next_parts/render_nudges.py',\n'desloppify/app/commands/next_parts/render_support.py',\n'desloppify/app/commands/plan/cluster_handlers.py',\n'desloppify/app/commands/plan/override_handlers.py',\n'desloppify/app/commands/plan/queue_render.py',\n'desloppify/app/commands/plan/commit_log_handlers.py',\n'desloppify/app/commands/resolve/render.py',\n'desloppify/app/commands/status_cmd.py',\n'desloppify/app/output/visualize.py',\n'desloppify/engine/_plan/commit_tracking.py',\n'desloppify/engine/_plan/schema_migrations.py',\n'desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py',\n'desloppify/engine/_plan/subjective_policy.py',\n'desloppify/engine/_scoring/subjective/core.py',\n'desloppify/engine/_work_queue/plan_order.py',\n'desloppify/engine/_work_queue/ranking.py',\n]\n\n# Build module path map for all repo py files\nmod_to_path = {}\npath_to_mod = {}\nfor py in root.rglob('*.py'):\n    rel = py.relative_to(root)\n    if rel.parts[0] != 'desloppify':\n        continue\n    mod = '.'.join(rel.with_suffix('').parts)\n    mod_to_path.setdefault(mod, []).append(rel)\n    path_to_mod[str(rel)] = mod\n\n# capture top-level imports by module\nmodule_level_imports = {}\nfor rel_str, mod in path_to_mod.items():\n    p = root / rel_str\n    try:\n        tree = ast.parse(p.read_text())\n    except Exception:\n        continue\n    imports=set()\n    for node in tree.body:\n        if isinstance(node, ast.Import):\n            for n in node.names:\n                imports.add(n.name)\n        elif isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom):\n            if node.module:\n                imports.add(node.module)\n    module_level_imports[mod]=imports\n\n\ndef resolve_from(module, level, current_mod):\n    if level==0:\n        return module or ''\n    parts=current_mod.split('.')\n    # current_mod includes module itself; relative import base is package\n    pkg=parts[:-1]\n    if level>len(pkg):\n        base=[]\n    else:\n        base=pkg[:len(pkg)-level+1]\n    if module:\n        return '.'.join(base+[module])\n    return '.'.join(base)\n\nfor rel in files:\n    p=root/rel\n    cur_mod='.'.join(Path(rel).with_suffix('').parts)\n    tree=ast.parse(p.read_text())\n    deferred=[]\n    class V(ast.NodeVisitor):\n        def __init__(self):\n            self.stack=[]\n        def visit_FunctionDef(self,node):\n            self.stack.append('f')\n            self.generic_visit(node)\n            self.stack.pop()\n        visit_AsyncFunctionDef=visit_FunctionDef\n        def visit_ClassDef(self,node):\n            self.stack.append('c')\n            self.generic_visit(node)\n            self.stack.pop()\n        def visit_Import(self,node):\n            if self.stack:\n                deferred.append((node.lineno,'import', [n.name for n in node.names], None, 0))\n        def visit_ImportFrom(self,node):\n            if self.stack:\n                deferred.append((node.lineno,'from', [n.name for n in node.names], node.module, node.level))\n    V().visit(tree)\n    if not deferred:\n        continue\n    print(f\"\\n{rel} ({cur_mod})\")\n    for lineno,kind,names,module,level in deferred:\n        if kind=='from':\n            tgt=resolve_from(module, level, cur_mod)\n            # check if target has module-level import of current module\n            cycle='unknown'\n            imports=module_level_imports.get(tgt)\n            if imports is not None:\n                cycle='YES' if cur_mod in imports else 'no'\n            print(f\"  L{lineno}: from {'.'*level + (module or '')} import {', '.join(names)} -> {tgt} | module-level back-import: {cycle}\")\n        else:\n            for n in names:\n                imports=module_level_imports.get(n)\n                cycle='unknown'\n                if imports is not None:\n                    cycle='YES' if cur_mod in imports else 'no'\n                print(f\"  L{lineno}: import {n} | module-level back-import: {cycle}\")\nPY"
}

{
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/concerns.py\n@@\n def _parse_complexity_signals(detail: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, float]:\n@@\n-    result: dict[str, float] = {}\n-    for sig in detail.get(\"complexity_signals\", []):\n+    result: dict[str, float] = {}\n+    raw_signals = detail.get(\"complexity_signals\", [])\n+    if not isinstance(raw_signals, list):\n+        return result\n+\n+    for sig in raw_signals:\n         if not isinstance(sig, str):\n             continue\n         m = re.search(r\"(\\d+)\\s*params\", sig)\n         if m:\n             result[\"max_params\"] = max(result.get(\"max_params\", 0), float(m.group(1)))\n@@\n def _extract_signals(issues: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> ConcernSignals:\n@@\n         det = f.get(\"detector\", \"\")\n         detail_raw = f.get(\"detail\", {})\n         detail = detail_raw if isinstance(detail_raw, dict) else {}\n+        legacy_raw = detail.get(\"signals\", {})\n+        legacy = legacy_raw if isinstance(legacy_raw, dict) else {}\n+        signal_src = legacy or detail\n \n         if det == \"structural\":\n-            # Read loc directly from the flat detail dict.\n-            _update_max_signal(signals, \"loc\", detail.get(\"loc\", 0))\n-            # Parse complexity_signals strings for params/nesting.\n+            # Support both legacy nested signals dict and flat structural detail.\n+            _update_max_signal(signals, \"loc\", signal_src.get(\"loc\", 0))\n+            _update_max_signal(signals, \"max_params\", signal_src.get(\"max_params\", 0))\n+            _update_max_signal(signals, \"max_nesting\", signal_src.get(\"max_nesting\", 0))\n+            # Parse complexity_signals labels for params/nesting.\n             parsed = _parse_complexity_signals(detail)\n             for key in (\"max_params\", \"max_nesting\"):\n                 if key in parsed:\n                     _update_max_signal(signals, cast(SignalKey, key), parsed[key])\n@@\n def _has_elevated_signals(issues: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> bool:\n@@\n         det = f.get(\"detector\", \"\")\n         detail_raw = f.get(\"detail\", {})\n         detail = detail_raw if isinstance(detail_raw, dict) else {}\n+        legacy_raw = detail.get(\"signals\", {})\n+        legacy = legacy_raw if isinstance(legacy_raw, dict) else {}\n+        signal_src = legacy or detail\n \n         if det == \"structural\":\n-            # Check flat detail keys first (real structural data).\n-            if detail.get(\"loc\", 0) >= ELEVATED_LOC:\n+            if signal_src.get(\"loc\", 0) >= ELEVATED_LOC:\n+                return True\n+            if signal_src.get(\"max_params\", 0) >= ELEVATED_MAX_PARAMS:\n+                return True\n+            if signal_src.get(\"max_nesting\", 0) >= ELEVATED_MAX_NESTING:\n                 return True\n             # Parse complexity_signals strings.\n             parsed = _parse_complexity_signals(detail)\n             if parsed.get(\"max_params\", 0) >= ELEVATED_MAX_PARAMS:\n                 return True\n*** End Patch\n"
}

Think through this practically, what does it do? Would we be breaing functonality?

> AGENT

I’ll break it down feature-by-feature against the current code paths so we can decide with low risk. I’m pulling the exact call sites now to show what D12 removal would actually change and what would break.

> AGENT

Short answer: **yes, D12 would break behavior today unless treated as an intentional feature removal/migration.**

Practically, D12 removes these capabilities:

1. **`promoted_ids`**
- Used when moving items to top in [operations_queue.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations_queue.py:130), and then used to keep promoted items ahead of injected subjective/triage items in [stale_dimensions.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py:205).
- Removing it changes queue ordering semantics (not a crash, but a real regression in priority behavior).  
- Tests assert this behavior in [test_stale_dimensions.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/plan/test_stale_dimensions.py:575).

2. **`permanent` / `false_positive` skip kinds**
- Exposed in CLI flags in [parser_groups_plan.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups_plan.py:117).
- Enforced/mapped in [skip_policy.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/skip_policy.py:5).
- Used for reopen semantics in [operations_skip.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations_skip.py:46).
- Removing them would directly break those user flows (intentional functionality loss).  
- Covered by tests in [test_skip.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/plan/test_skip.py:60).

3. **Per-issue override `description` / `note`**
- Written by `plan describe` / `plan note` in [override_handlers.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/override_handlers.py:103).
- Stored via [operations_meta.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations_meta.py:50).
- Removing these fields breaks those commands’ persistence.
- Also, `overrides` itself cannot be removed: cluster links are stored there in [operations_cluster.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations_cluster.py:60).

Current data snapshot:
- `promoted_ids` is empty right now.
- skips are all `temporary` right now.
- `description`/`note` currently unused in overrides.
- But these are still live features in code/CLI/tests.

Recommendation: **keep D12 deferred** unless you explicitly want to remove these features and accept behavior/CLI changes.

> DEVELOPER

Is D12 code ugly?

> AGENT

Not especially.

It’s mostly coherent and intentional, but it’s “product complexity,” not accidental mess:
1. `promoted_ids`: small and clear, but adds queue-order policy branching.
2. skip kinds (`temporary/permanent/false_positive`): explicit and well-factored, but expands CLI/state transitions.
3. override metadata (`description`/`note`): straightforward; low ugliness, moderate surface area.

So D12 is less “ugly code” and more “optional feature footprint.” Removing it is a product decision, not a code-health necessity.
